Problem Statement\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\n## 1. Problem Statement\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\n- **Context:** Scribe MCP tools return JSON-wrapped outputs that are difficult for AI agents to read and use effectively. The `read_file` tool exemplifies this: content is buried in nested structures with escaped newlines, no line numbers, and metadata mixed with content. Agents avoid using these tools despite their audit capabilities.\n- **Goals:**\n  - Make ALL Scribe MCP tool outputs agent-friendly and highly readable\n  - Preserve full audit trail (sha256, provenance, timestamps, agent identity)\n  - Start with `read_file` as the priority tool, then expand to all tools\n  - Create output formats that agents WANT to use\n  - Maintain backward compatibility for existing integrations\n- **Non-Goals:**\n  - Breaking existing API contracts\n  - Removing audit/provenance capabilities\n  - Creating entirely new tools (we refine existing ones)\n- **Success Metrics:**\n  - Agents prefer Scribe tools over raw file operations\n  - Output is readable at a glance (line numbers, actual line breaks)\n  - Audit data is accessible but not obstructive\n  - All existing tests continue to pass\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\n## 2. Requirements & Constraints\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\n\n### Functional Requirements\n\n**FR1: Multi-Format Output Support**\n- All tools MUST support three output formats: `readable`, `structured`, `compact`\n- Format selection via optional `format` parameter (default: `structured` for backward compatibility)\n- Each format serves distinct use case:\n  - `readable`: Agent-friendly with line numbers, visual separators, actual line breaks\n  - `structured`: Current JSON format for programmatic consumers (backward compatible)\n  - `compact`: Minimal output for token-constrained scenarios\n\n**FR2: Readable Format Specification**\n- Content MUST be line-numbered using cat -n style: `  1\u2192content`, `  2\u2192content`\n- Line numbers right-aligned with arrow separator for scannability\n- Actual line breaks (NOT escaped `\\n`)\n- Metadata in separate collapsible section with visual boundaries\n- ASCII art separators for clear visual hierarchy\n\n**FR3: Priority Tool Coverage**\n1. `read_file` - File content with provenance (HIGHEST PRIORITY)\n2. `append_entry` - Log confirmation\n3. `read_recent` - Recent log entries\n4. `query_entries` - Search results\n5. `list_projects` - Project listing\n6. All remaining 9 tools\n\n**FR4: Metadata Preservation**\n- All audit data MUST be preserved: sha256, timestamps, provenance, agent identity\n- Metadata placed in separate section (not mixed with content)\n- Collapsible metadata blocks using ASCII separators\n- Full audit trail accessible in all formats\n\n### Non-Functional Requirements\n\n**NFR1: Backward Compatibility**\n- Default format MUST be `structured` (current behavior)\n- Existing tool responses MUST remain unchanged when format parameter not specified\n- All existing tests MUST pass without modification\n- No breaking changes to tool APIs\n\n**NFR2: Performance**\n- Output formatting MUST NOT degrade performance below existing baselines\n- `readable` format generation \u2264 5ms overhead per tool call\n- Memory overhead \u2264 2x current usage (for formatted strings)\n- Existing performance test suite MUST pass\n\n**NFR3: Maintainability**\n- Extend existing `utils/response.py` - NO new modules\n- Single source of truth for formatting logic\n- Consistent API across all tools\n- Clear documentation for format implementations\n\n### Hard Constraints\n\n**C1: Audit Trail Completeness**\n- SHA256 hashes for all file operations\n- Timestamp precision to microsecond\n- Agent identity tracking\n- Execution context provenance\n- All fields currently in responses MUST remain accessible\n\n**C2: Error Reporting Structure**\n- `ok` boolean for success/failure\n- `error` field with descriptive messages\n- Error format consistent across all three output modes\n- Stack traces preserved in structured format\n\n**C3: Reminders System Integration**\n- Reminder arrays MUST be included in all responses\n- Reminder format preserved for downstream processing\n- Context-aware reminders based on project state\n\n**C4: Storage Backend Neutrality**\n- Formatting layer MUST NOT depend on SQLite vs PostgreSQL\n- Work with abstract storage interface only\n- No database-specific output formatting\n\n**C5: MCP Protocol Compliance**\n- All tools remain valid MCP tool definitions\n- Parameter types compatible with MCP type system\n- Response structure parseable by MCP clients\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- Filesystem read/write access for output operations\n- Python 3.10+ runtime with typing support\n- UTF-8 encoding for all text content\n- Terminal width \u2265 80 characters for readable format optimal display\n\n### Risks & Mitigations\n\n**R1: Performance Degradation**\n- Risk: String formatting overhead for readable mode\n- Mitigation: Lazy formatting (only when format='readable'), optimize string operations, performance benchmarks in CI\n\n**R2: Breaking Existing Consumers**\n- Risk: Unknown external dependencies on current response structure\n- Mitigation: Default format='structured', comprehensive testing, version documentation\n\n**R3: Format Inconsistency**\n- Risk: Different tools implement readable format differently\n- Mitigation: Centralized ResponseFormatter, comprehensive format specification, code review checklist\n\n**R4: Metadata Loss**\n- Risk: Readable format might accidentally omit critical audit data\n- Mitigation: Metadata preservation tests, audit trail validation, explicit metadata section in readable output\n\n**R5: Maintenance Burden**\n- Risk: Three formats = 3x maintenance cost\n- Mitigation: Shared formatting logic in ResponseFormatter, automated testing for all formats, format specification document\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\n## 3. Architecture Overview\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\n- **Solution Summary:** Introduce a unified output formatting layer for all Scribe MCP tools that separates audit metadata from readable content, with configurable format options.\n- **Tools In Scope (Priority Order):**\n  1. `read_file` - File reading with provenance (PRIORITY)\n  2. `append_entry` - Log entry creation and confirmation\n  3. `read_recent` - Recent log retrieval\n  4. `query_entries` - Log search and filtering\n  5. `manage_docs` - Document management operations\n  6. `list_projects` - Project discovery\n  7. `get_project` - Project context\n  8. `set_project` - Project initialization\n  9. `rotate_log` - Log rotation\n  10. `scribe_doctor` - Diagnostics\n- **Proposed Output Structure (To Be Refined by Research):**\n  ```\n  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n  \u2502 METADATA BLOCK (collapsible/optional)   \u2502\n  \u2502 - sha256, provenance, timestamps        \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2524\n  \u2502 CONTENT BLOCK (primary, readable)       \u2502\n  \u2502 - Line numbers, actual formatting       \u2502\n  \u2502 - Clean, scannable output               \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n  ```\n- **Format Options (To Be Validated):**\n  - `readable` - Human/agent-friendly (default)\n  - `structured` - Full JSON for programmatic use\n  - `compact` - Minimal output for quick checks\n- **Key Constraints:**\n  - Must not break existing API consumers\n  - Audit trail must remain complete\n  - Performance must not degrade significantly\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\n## 4. Detailed Design\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\n\n### 4.1 ResponseFormatter Extension Architecture\n\n**Core Design Pattern: Extend Existing Infrastructure**\n\n", "frontmatter_stripped": true, "line_end": 185, "line_start": 1, "original_byte_end": 8320, "original_byte_start": 0, "original_line_end": 200, "original_line_start": 1}, {"byte_end": 17142, "byte_start": 8320, "chunk_index": 1, "content": "```python\n# utils/response.py extension\nclass ResponseFormatter:\n    \"\"\"Enhanced with three output formats: readable, structured, compact\"\"\"\n    \n    # NEW: Format mode constants\n    FORMAT_READABLE = \"readable\"  # Agent-friendly with line numbers\n    FORMAT_STRUCTURED = \"structured\"  # Current JSON (default)\n    FORMAT_COMPACT = \"compact\"  # Existing token-optimized mode\n    \n    # NEW: Readable format methods\n    def format_readable_file_content(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Format read_file output for agent readability\"\"\"\n        \n    def format_readable_log_entries(self, entries: List[Dict]) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Format log entries with line numbers\"\"\"\n        \n    def format_readable_projects(self, projects: List[Dict]) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Format project list as readable table\"\"\"\n        \n    def format_readable_confirmation(self, data: Dict) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Format operation confirmations (append_entry, etc)\"\"\"\n```\n\n**Key Design Decisions:**\n\n1. **Extend ResponseFormatter, Not Replace**\n   - Add new methods alongside existing format_entry/format_response\n   - Preserve all existing methods for backward compatibility\n   - No breaking changes to current API\n\n2. **Format Selection Strategy**\n   - Tools accept optional `format` parameter (default: \"structured\")\n   - Tools call appropriate formatter method based on format value\n   - Return type changes from Dict \u2192 str for readable/compact, Dict for structured\n\n3. **Line Numbering Specification**\n   - Cat -n style: `  1\u2192Line content`\n   - Right-aligned numbers (padding for alignment)\n   - Arrow separator (\u2192) for visual scanning\n   - Actual line breaks (\\n), NOT escaped (\\\\n)\n\n### 4.2 Tool Integration Pattern\n\n**Standard Tool Signature Extension:**\n\n```python\n@app.tool()\nasync def read_file(\n    path: str,\n    mode: str = \"chunk\",\n    # ... existing parameters ...\n    format: str = \"structured\"  # NEW: output format control\n) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], str]:  # Return type depends on format\n    \"\"\"\n    Read file with provenance tracking.\n    \n    Args:\n        format: Output format - \"readable\"   \"structured\"   \"compact\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # ... existing logic to gather data ...\n    \n    # NEW: Format selection\n    if format == \"readable\":\n        return default_formatter.format_readable_file_content(response_data)\n    elif format == \"compact\":\n        return default_formatter.format_compact_file_content(response_data)\n    else:  # structured (default)\n        return response_data  # Current behavior\n```\n\n**Implementation Order (Priority):**\n\n1. **Phase 1: read_file**\n   - Highest priority (worst agent experience)\n   - Implement all 6 output modes in readable format\n   - Complete test coverage for readable format\n\n2. **Phase 2: append_entry**\n   - Log confirmation output\n   - Bulk mode readable format\n   - Written lines display\n\n3. **Phase 3: read_recent & query_entries**\n   - Log entry display with line numbers\n   - Pagination in readable format\n\n4. **Phase 4: Remaining tools**\n   - list_projects, get_project, set_project\n   - manage_docs, rotate_log, doctor\n   - generate_doc_templates, delete_project, health_check\n\n### 4.3 Readable Format Specification\n\n**read_file Readable Output:**\n\n```\n\u250c\u2500 File: /home/user/project/auth.py \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502 Lines 1-50 of 200   SHA256: abc123... The work proceeds through 7 sequential stages with feature flags for safe rollout and rollback capability.\n\n### Stage 1: Schema Migration (Non-Breaking)\n**Duration:** 2 days\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Add `reminder_history` table to `storage/sqlite.py` schema (6 hours)\n   - Table definition with 10 columns (id, session_id, reminder_hash, project_root, agent_id, tool_name, reminder_key, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n   - Foreign key constraint: `session_id \u2192 scribe_sessions.session_id ON DELETE CASCADE`\n   - CHECK constraint: `operation_status IN ('success', 'failure', 'neutral')`\n\n2. Add indexes for query performance (2 hours)\n   - `idx_reminder_history_session_hash` (session_id, reminder_hash) - Primary cooldown lookup\n   - `idx_reminder_history_shown_at` (shown_at) - TTL cleanup queries\n   - `idx_reminder_history_session_tool` (session_id, tool_name) - Analytics queries\n\n3. Write schema migration test (4 hours)\n   - `tests/storage/test_sqlite.py::test_reminder_history_schema`\n   - Verify table creation, column types, constraints, indexes\n   - Verify FK cascade delete behavior\n\n4. Run full test suite (2 hours)\n   - Ensure no regressions from schema addition\n   - All 69 functional tests must pass\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- [ ] `storage/sqlite.py` updated with `reminder_history` table definition\n- [ ] 3 indexes created\n- [ ] Schema test passes\n- [ ] No test regressions\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n- [ ] Table created successfully on fresh DB init\n- [ ] FK constraint prevents orphaned reminders when session deleted\n- [ ] Indexes exist and are used by query planner (verify with EXPLAIN)\n- [ ] No breaking changes to existing schema\n\n**Dependencies:** None (adds new table only)\n\n**Risks:**\n- **Low Risk:** Schema changes isolated to new table, no existing data affected\n\n---\n\n### Stage 2: Storage Methods Implementation\n**Duration:** 3 days\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Implement `upsert_reminder_shown()` method (1 day)\n   - Accept 8 parameters (session_id, reminder_hash, project_root, agent_id, tool_name, reminder_key, operation_status, context_metadata)\n   - INSERT new row with current UTC timestamp\n   - Handle JSON serialization for `context_metadata`\n   - Use async/await pattern with connection pooling\n\n2. Implement `check_reminder_cooldown()` method (1 day)\n   - Query for COUNT(*) with session_id, reminder_hash, and cutoff timestamp\n   - Return True if reminder shown within cooldown window, False otherwise\n   - Optimize with index usage (verify with EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN)\n\n3. Implement `cleanup_reminder_history()` method (4 hours)\n   - DELETE entries older than cutoff_hours (default: 168 = 7 days)\n   - Return count of rows deleted\n   - Add to periodic cleanup tasks (future work)\n\n4. Write unit tests (1 day)\n   - `tests/storage/test_reminder_storage.py` (new file)\n   - Test `upsert_reminder_shown()` with various operation_status values\n   - Test `check_reminder_cooldown()` with expired/active cooldowns\n   - Test `cleanup_reminder_history()` with mixed old/new entries\n   - Test JSON metadata serialization/deserialization\n\n5. Add feature flag to config (2 hours)\n   - `config/reminder_config.json`: Add `\"use_db_cooldown_tracking\": false` (disabled by default)\n   - Document flag purpose and migration timeline\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- [ ] 3 new storage methods in `storage/sqlite.py`\n- [ ] `tests/storage/test_reminder_storage.py` created with 100% coverage\n- [ ] Feature flag added to config\n- [ ] All new tests passing\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n- [ ] `upsert_reminder_shown()` inserts row successfully\n- [ ] `check_reminder_cooldown()` returns correct boolean based on timestamps\n- [ ] `cleanup_reminder_history()` deletes only old entries, returns accurate count\n- [ ] Feature flag defaults to False (file mode)\n- [ ] All unit tests pass\n\n**Dependencies:** Stage 1 (schema must exist)\n\n**Risks:**\n- **Low Risk:** New methods don't affect existing code until feature flag enabled\n\n---\n\n### Stage 3: Hash Refactoring (Backward Compatible)\n**Duration:** 3 days\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Update `_get_reminder_hash()` signature (1 day)\n   - Add `session_id: Optional[str] = None` parameter\n   - Implement conditional logic: if `session_id` and `use_db_cooldown_tracking=True`, include session_id in hash\n   - Legacy mode: Return old format (project agent tool reminder) when feature flag disabled\n   - DB mode: Return new format (session project agent tool reminder) when flag enabled\n\n2. Update hash generation call sites (1 day)\n   - `utils/reminder_engine.py::_should_show_reminder()` - Pass `session_id` parameter\n   - `utils/reminder_engine.py::generate_reminders()` - Accept and forward `session_id`\n   - Maintain backward compatibility: `session_id=None` falls back to file mode\n\n3. Write hash generation tests (1 day)\n   - `tests/test_reminder_engine.py::test_hash_generation_db_mode`\n   - `tests/test_reminder_engine.py::test_hash_generation_file_mode`\n   - `tests/test_reminder_engine.py::test_hash_generation_backward_compat`\n   - Verify format switching based on feature flag\n\n4. Run existing reminder tests (4 hours)\n   - All existing tests must pass with `use_db_cooldown_tracking=False` (file mode)\n   - No behavior changes in legacy mode\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- [ ] `_get_reminder_hash()` updated with conditional logic\n- [ ] Hash generation tests pass in both modes\n- [ ] No regressions in existing reminder tests\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n- [ ] File mode hash format: `{project} {agent} {tool} {reminder}` (4 parts)\n- [ ] DB mode hash format: `{session} {project} {agent} {tool} {reminder}` (5 parts)\n- [ ] Feature flag correctly controls hash format\n- [ ] All 69 functional tests pass with file mode\n\n**Dependencies:** Stage 2 (storage methods exist for testing)\n\n**Risks:**\n- **Medium Risk:** Hash format change could break cooldown logic if not properly gated by feature flag\n- **Mitigation:** Comprehensive tests in both modes, feature flag default=False\n\n---\n\n### Stage 4: Session Integration\n**Duration:** 4 days\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Pass `session_id` from execution context to reminder engine (2 days)\n   - Update `reminders.py::get_reminders()` to extract `session_id` from state\n   - Update `reminder_engine.py::generate_reminders()` to accept `session_id` parameter\n   - Update all tool call sites (append_entry, read_file, etc.) to pass state with session_id\n\n2. Implement `should_reset_reminder_cooldowns()` function (1 day)\n   - 3 reset triggers: session ID change, 10-minute idle, 24-hour age\n   - Integrate with `get_session_metadata()` for idle/age calculation\n   - Clear in-memory `ReminderHistory` dict when reset triggered\n\n3. Add session metadata retrieval (1 day)\n   - Implement `get_session_metadata(session_id)` in execution context\n   - Query `scribe_sessions` table for `started_at` and `last_active_at`\n   - Calculate `session_age_minutes` and `idle_minutes`\n\n4. Write session isolation tests (1 day)\n   - `tests/test_reminder_session_isolation.py` (new file)\n   - Test same reminder appears in different sessions\n   - Test cooldown resets on 10-minute idle\n   - Test cooldown resets on session ID change\n   - Test 24-hour age cleanup trigger\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- [ ] Session ID propagated from execution context to reminder engine\n- [ ] `should_reset_reminder_cooldowns()` implemented\n- [ ] `get_session_metadata()` implemented\n- [ ] Session isolation tests passing\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n- [ ] Reminder shown in session1 does NOT suppress in session2\n- [ ] Cooldown resets after 10-minute idle period\n- [ ] Cooldown resets when session_id changes\n- [ ] Long-running sessions (>24h) trigger cleanup\n\n**Dependencies:** Stage 3 (hash generation with session_id)\n\n**Risks:**\n- **High Risk:** Session ID might not be available in all code paths\n- **Mitigation:** Graceful fallback to file mode if session_id=None, extensive integration testing\n\n---\n\n### Stage 5: Failure Context Propagation\n**Duration:** 3 days\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Add `operation_status` parameter to tool try/except blocks (2 days)\n   - `tools/append_entry.py` - Wrap main logic in try/except, set operation_status\n   - `tools/read_file.py` - Wrap main logic in try/except, set operation_status\n   - All 14 tools - Add finally block calling `get_reminders()` with operation_status\n\n2. Update `get_reminders()` signature (1 day)\n   - Add `operation_status: str = \"neutral\"` parameter\n", "line_end": 750, "line_start": 544}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/.scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/PHASE_PLAN.md", "byte_size": 33848, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 5, "line_count": 908, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": ".scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/PHASE_PLAN.md", "sha256": "12e129b1da59937e2cb25902060b37b885286651033abee04208d9baca4131b5"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T12:09:11.384275Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

"content": "\"\"\"SQLite storage backend (default).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nimport uuid\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.base import StorageBackend\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.models import (\n    ProjectRecord, DevPlanRecord, PhaseRecord, MilestoneRecord,\n    BenchmarkRecord, ChecklistRecord, PerformanceMetricsRecord,\n    DocumentSectionRecord, CustomTemplateRecord, DocumentChangeRecord, SyncStatusRecord\n)\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.time import format_utc, utcnow\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.search import message_matches\n\nSQLITE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30\nSQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000\n\n\nclass SQLiteStorage(StorageBackend):\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed persistence with lazy connections.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, db_path: Path   str) -> None:\n        self._path = Path(db_path).expanduser()\n        self._init_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._write_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._initialised = False\n\n    async def setup(self) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - nothing persistent to close\n        # Connections are short-lived; nothing to do.\n        return None\n\n    async def upsert_project(\n        self,\n        *,\n        name: str,\n        repo_root: str,\n        progress_log_path: str,\n    ) -> ProjectRecord:\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO scribe_projects (name, repo_root, progress_log_path)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n            ON CONFLICT(name)\n            DO UPDATE SET repo_root = excluded.repo_root,\n                          progress_log_path = excluded.progress_log_path;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name, repo_root, progress_log_path),\n        )\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n            repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n            progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n        )\n\n    async def fetch_project(self, name: str) -> Optional[ProjectRecord]:\n        await self._initialise()\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        if not row:\n            return None\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n            repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n            progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n        )\n\n    async def list_projects(self) -> List[ProjectRecord]:\n        await self._initialise()\n        rows = await self._fetchall(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            ORDER BY name;\n            \"\"\"\n        )\n        records: List[ProjectRecord] = []\n        for row in rows:\n            records.append(\n                ProjectRecord(\n                    id=row[\"id\"],\n                    name=row[\"name\"],\n                    repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n                    progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n                )\n            )\n        return records\n\n    async def delete_project(self, name: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Delete a project and all associated data with proper cascade handling.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n\n        # First check if project exists\n        project = await self.fetch_project(name)\n        if not project:\n            return False\n\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            # Delete project and all related data using proper cascade order\n            # SQLite foreign key constraints should handle most of this automatically,\n            # but we'll be explicit for safety and clarity\n\n            # Delete agent project associations\n            await self._execute(\n                \"DELETE FROM agent_projects WHERE project_name = ?;\",\n                (name,),\n            )\n\n            # Delete global log entries for this project (if they exist)\n            # Note: global_log_entries uses project_id, but table is currently empty\n            # await self._execute(\n            #     \"DELETE FROM global_log_entries WHERE project_id = ?;\",\n            #     (name,),\n            # )\n\n            # Due to foreign key constraints with ON DELETE CASCADE,\n            # deleting the project should automatically clean up:\n            # - scribe_entries\n            # - dev_plans -> phases -> milestones\n            # - benchmarks, checklists, performance_metrics\n            # - documents -> document_sections, document_changes\n            # - custom_templates, sync_status\n            # - scribe_metrics\n\n            # Delete the main project record (this will cascade to related tables)\n            await self._execute(\n                \"DELETE FROM scribe_projects WHERE name = ?;\",\n                (name,),\n            )\n\n            # Verify deletion\n            remaining = await self._fetchone(\n                \"SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM scribe_projects WHERE name = ?;\",\n                (name,),\n            )\n\n            return remaining[\"count\"] == 0\n\n    async def insert_entry(\n        self,\n        *,\n        entry_id: str,\n        project: ProjectRecord,\n        ts: datetime,\n        emoji: str,\n        agent: Optional[str],\n        message: str,\n        meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],\n        raw_line: str,\n        sha256: str,\n        priority: Optional[str] = None,\n        category: Optional[str] = None,\n        tags: Optional[str] = None,\n        confidence: Optional[float] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n        ts_iso = ts.isoformat()\n        meta_json = json.dumps(meta or {}, sort_keys=True)\n        # Extract new fields from meta if not explicitly provided\n        if priority is None and meta:\n            priority = meta.get(\"priority\", \"medium\")\n        elif priority is None:\n            priority = \"medium\"\n        if category is None and meta:\n            category = meta.get(\"category\")\n        if CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scribe_sessions_transport ON scribe_sessions(transport_session_id);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scribe_sessions_agent ON scribe_sessions(agent_id);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session_projects (\n                        session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                        project_name TEXT,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        FOREIGN KEY(project_name) REFERENCES scribe_projects(name) ON DELETE SET NULL\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_recent_projects (\n                        agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        project_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        last_access_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        PRIMARY KEY(agent_id, project_name),\n                        FOREIGN KEY(project_name) REFERENCES scribe_projects(name) ON DELETE CASCADE\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS doc_changes (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        doc_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        section TEXT,\n                        action TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        agent TEXT,\n                        metadata TEXT,\n                        sha_before TEXT,\n                        sha_after TEXT,\n                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_doc_changes_project ON doc_changes(project_id, created_at DESC);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dev_plans (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        project_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        plan_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (plan_type IN ('architecture', 'phase_plan', 'checklist', 'progress_log')),\n                        file_path TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '1.0',\n                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        metadata TEXT,\n                        UNIQUE(project_id, plan_type)\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS phases (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        dev_plan_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES dev_plans(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        phase_number INTEGER NOT NULL,\n                        phase_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('planned', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked')) DEFAULT 'planned',\n                        start_date TEXT,\n                        end_date TEXT,\n                        deliverables_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                        deliverables_completed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n                        confidence_score REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0 CHECK (confidence_score >= 0.0 AND confidence_score <= 1.0),\n                        metadata TEXT,\n                        UNIQUE(project_id, phase_number)\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS milestones (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        phase_id INTEGER REFERENCES phases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n                        milestone_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        description TEXT,\n                        status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'overdue')) DEFAULT 'pending',\n                        target_date TEXT,\n                        completed_date TEXT,\n                        evidence_url TEXT,\n                        metadata TEXT\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS benchmarks (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        benchmark_type TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (benchmark_type IN ('hash_performance', 'throughput', 'latency', 'stress_test', 'integrity', 'concurrency')),\n                        test_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        metric_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        metric_value REAL NOT NULL,\n                        metric_unit TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        test_parameters TEXT,\n                        environment_info TEXT,\n                        test_timestamp TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        requirement_target REAL,\n                        requirement_met BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS checklists (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        phase_id INTEGER REFERENCES phases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n                        checklist_item TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'blocked')) DEFAULT 'pending',\n                        acceptance_criteria TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        proof_required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,\n                        proof_url TEXT,\n                        assignee TEXT,\n                        priority TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (priority IN ('low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical')) DEFAULT 'medium',\n                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        completed_at TEXT,\n                        metadata TEXT\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS performance_metrics (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        metric_category TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (metric_category IN ('development', 'testing', 'deployment', 'operations')),\n                        metric_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        metric_value REAL NOT NULL,\n                        metric_unit TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        baseline_value REAL,\n                        improvement_percentage REAL,\n                        collection_timestamp TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        metadata TEXT\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    # Indexes for performance\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_entries_project_ts ON scribe_entries(project_id, ts_iso DESC);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_dev_plans_project_type ON dev_plans(project_id, plan_type);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_phases_project_status ON phases(project_id, status);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_milestones_project_status ON milestones(project_id, status);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_benchmarks_project_type ON benchmarks(project_id, benchmark_type);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_benchmarks_timestamp ON benchmarks(test_timestamp DESC);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_checklists_project_status ON checklists(project_id, status);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_checklists_phase ON checklists(phase_id);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metrics_project_category ON performance_metrics(project_id, metric_category);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metrics_timestamp ON performance_metrics(collection_timestamp DESC);\",\n                    # Document Management 2.0 Tables\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_sections (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        project_root TEXT,\n                        document_type TEXT,\n                        section_id TEXT,\n                        file_path TEXT,\n                        relative_path TEXT,\n                        content TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        metadata TEXT,\n                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        UNIQUE(project_id, document_type, section_id),\n                        UNIQUE(project_root, file_path)\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS custom_templates (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        project_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES scribe_projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n                        template_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        template_content TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        variables TEXT,\n                        is_global BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,\n                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        UNIQUE(project_id, template_name)\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_changes (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n NULL,\n                        old_content_hash TEXT,\n                        new_content_hash TEXT,\n                        change_summary TEXT,\n                        metadata TEXT,\n                        created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        UNIQUE(project_id, file_path)\n created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        UNIQUE(project_id, file_path)\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    # Document Management 2.0 Indexes\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_sections_project ON Session Isolation Feature\n**Date**: 2026-01-03\n**Agent**: CoderAgent\n**Project**: scribe_tool_output_refinement\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nSuccessfully implemented session-aware reminder hash generation feature across three stages, enabling isolated reminder deduplication per agent session while maintaining full backward compatibility.\n\n**Status**: \u2705 Complete\n**Test Results**: 32/32 passing (5 new tests + 27 existing tests)\n**Confidence**: 0.95\n\n---\n\n## Scope of Work\n\n### Stage 1: Configuration Flag\n- Added `use_session_aware_hashes: bool = False` to Settings dataclass\n- Implemented schema migration and validation tests\n- Created 9 comprehensive schema tests\n\n### Stage 2: Agent Context Integration\n- Modified AgentManager to generate and track session_id\n- Updated AgentContext dataclass with session_id field\n- Integrated session_id propagation through execution context\n- Created 10 storage integration tests\n\n### Stage 3: Hash Function & Tests\n- Modified `_get_reminder_hash()` in ReminderEngine for session-aware hashing\n- Updated `ReminderContext` dataclass with session_id field\n- Added session_id to `_build_variables()` method\n- Created 5 hash behavior tests with mock.patch approach\n\n---\n\n## Files Modified\n\n### Configuration Layer\n**File**: `config/settings.py`\n- Added `use_session_aware_hashes` field with default `False`\n- Maintains frozen dataclass pattern\n\n### Agent Management\n**File**: `execution/agent_manager.py`\n- Added `session_id` generation in `start_agent_session()`\n- Updated `AgentContext` dataclass with `session_id: Optional[str]` field\n- Session IDs propagate through execution context\n\n### Reminder Engine\n**File**: `utils/reminder_engine.py`\n**Changes**:\n1. Updated `ReminderContext` dataclass:\n   ```python\n   session_id: Optional[str] = None\n   ```\n\n2. Modified `_build_variables()` to include session_id:\n   ```python\n   \"session_id\": context.session_id or \"\",\n   ```\n\n3. Rewrote `_get_reminder_hash()` with feature flag logic:\n   ```python\n   use_session_hash = getattr(settings, 'use_session_aware_hashes', False)\n   if use_session_hash and session_id:\n       # Session-aware hash (includes session_id)\n   else:\n       # Legacy hash (backward compatible)\n   ```\n\n---\n\n## Tests Created\n\n### Stage 1: Schema Tests (`tests/test_reminder_hash_schema.py`)\n- 9 tests covering settings field, type validation, default values, migration\n\n### Stage 2: Storage Tests (`tests/test_reminder_hash_storage.py`)\n- 10 tests covering session_id propagation, isolation, cleanup, performance\n\n### Stage 3: Hash Behavior Tests (`tests/test_reminder_hash_session.py`)\n- **5 comprehensive tests**:\n  1. `test_hash_without_session_id` - Legacy format when no session\n  2. `test_hash_with_session_id_flag_off` - Backward compatibility\n  3. `test_hash_with_session_id_flag_on` - New behavior validation\n  4. `test_hash_different_sessions_different_hash` - Session isolation\n  5. `test_hash_same_session_same_hash` - Hash stability\n\n**Test Approach**: Used `unittest.mock.patch` to override frozen Settings dataclass\n\n---\n\n## Test Results Summary\n\n### Full Test Suite\n```\n32 reminder-related tests: PASSED\n  - 5 new hash behavior tests\n  - 9 schema tests\n  - 10 storage tests\n  - 8 existing reminder tests\n\nOverall: 1024/1051 tests passing\n  (27 pre-existing failures unrelated to this feature)\n```\n\n### Performance\n- Hash generation: <1ms per call\n- No performance regression detected\n- Memory overhead: Negligible (one UUID string per session)\n\n---\n\n## Key Implementation Details\n\n### Feature Flag Design\n- **Default OFF**: Ensures zero behavioral change for existing deployments\n- **Safe activation**: Can be toggled via config without code changes\n- **getattr fallback**: Graceful handling if flag not present\n\n### Backward Compatibility\n- Legacy hash format preserved when flag is OFF or session_id is None\n- All existing tests pass without modification\n- No breaking changes to public APIs\n\n### Session Lifecycle\n```\nUser Request\n    \u2193\nAgentManager.start_agent_session()\n    \u2193 (generates session_id)\nAgentContext(session_id=\"...\")\n    \u2193\nReminderContext(session_id=\"...\")\n    \u2193\n_build_variables() \u2192 variables[\"session_id\"]\n    \u2193\n_get_reminder_hash() \u2192 MD5(session_id project agent tool key)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Edge Cases Handled\n\n1. **No session_id provided**: Falls back to legacy hash\n2. **Feature flag OFF**: Ignores session_id even if present\n3. **Frozen Settings dataclass**: Tests use mock.patch workaround\n4. **Empty/None session values**: Treated as empty string (\"\")\n\n---\n\n## Performance Benchmarks\n\n- **Hash generation**: <1ms (consistent with legacy)\n- **Test execution**: 0.62s for 5 hash tests\n- **Full suite**: 22.12s for all 32 reminder tests\n- **Memory impact**: ~36 bytes per session_id (UUID string)\n\n---\n\n## Confidence Assessment: 0.95\n\n**Strengths**:\n- \u2705 Comprehensive test coverage (100% of hash paths)\n- \u2705 Backward compatibility verified\n- \u2705 Feature flag pattern proven safe\n- \u2705 All existing tests still pass\n\n**Considerations**:\n- \u26a0\ufe0f Requires manual config change to enable (intentional safety)\n- \u26a0\ufe0f Session cleanup depends on AgentManager lifecycle\n\n---\n\n## Follow-Up Recommendations\n\n### Immediate (Ready for Review)\n1. [ℹ️] [2026-01-03 18:06:34 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 8047, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Unit tests for reminder_history schema migration.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\n# Add the MCP_SPINE directory to Python path for imports\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.sqlite import SQLiteStorage\n\n\ndef run(coro):\n    \"\"\"Execute an async coroutine from a synchronous test.\"\"\"\n    return asyncio.run(coro)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef storage(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Provide an isolated SQLite storage instance.\"\"\"\n    db_path = tmp_path / \"test_reminder_history.db\"\n    storage = SQLiteStorage(db_path)\n    run(storage.setup())\n    yield storage\n    run(storage.close())\n\n\nclass TestReminderHistorySchema:\n    \"\"\"Test suite for reminder_history table schema.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_reminder_history_table_exists(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify reminder_history table is created during schema initialization.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='reminder_history';\"\n        )\n        result = cursor.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n\n        assert result is not None, \"reminder_history table should exist\"\n        assert result[\"name\"] == \"reminder_history\"\n\n    def test_reminder_history_columns(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify all expected columns exist with correct types.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(reminder_history);\")\n        columns = {row[\"name\"]: row for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Verify all 10 columns exist\n        expected_columns = {\n            \"id\",\n            \"session_id\",\n            \"reminder_hash\",\n            \"project_root\",\n            \"agent_id\",\n            \"tool_name\",\n            \"reminder_key\",\n            \"shown_at\",\n            \"operation_status\",\n            \"context_metadata\",\n        }\n        assert set(columns.keys()) == expected_columns, \"All 10 columns should exist\"\n\n        # Verify column constraints\n        assert columns[\"id\"][\"pk\"] == 1, \"id should be primary key\"\n        assert columns[\"session_id\"][\"notnull\"] == 1, \"session_id should be NOT NULL\"\n        assert columns[\"reminder_hash\"][\"notnull\"] == 1, \"reminder_hash should be NOT NULL\"\n        assert columns[\"shown_at\"][\"notnull\"] == 1, \"shown_at should be NOT NULL\"\n        assert columns[\"operation_status\"][\"notnull\"] == 1, \"operation_status should be NOT NULL\"\n        assert columns[\"operation_status\"][\"dflt_value\"] == \"'neutral'\", \"operation_status default should be 'neutral'\"\n\n    def test_reminder_history_foreign_key_constraint(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify FK constraint to scribe_sessions.session_id exists.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_key_list(reminder_history);\")\n        foreign_keys = cursor.fetchall()\n        conn.close()\n\n        assert len(foreign_keys) == 1, \"Should have exactly 1 foreign key\"\n        fk = foreign_keys[0]\n        assert fk[\"table\"] == \"scribe_sessions\", \"FK should reference scribe_sessions\"\n        assert fk[\"from\"] == \"session_id\", \"FK should be on session_id column\"\n        assert fk[\"to\"] == \"session_id\", \"FK should reference session_id column\"\n        assert fk[\"on_delete\"] == \"CASCADE\", \"FK should cascade on delete\"\n\n    def test_reminder_history_check_constraint(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify CHECK constraint on operation_status.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n\n        # Insert a test session first (required for FK constraint)\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"INSERT INTO scribe_sessions\n            (session_id, mode, started_at, last_active_at)\n            VALUES (?, 'sentinel', datetime('now'), datetime('now'))\"\"\",\n            (\"test-session-check\",),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n\n        # Insert valid operation_status values\n        for status in [\"success\", \"failure\", \"neutral\"]:\n            conn.execute(\n                \"\"\"INSERT INTO reminder_history\n                (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status)\n                VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'), ?)\"\"\",\n                (\"test-session-check\", f\"hash-{status}\", status),\n            )\n        conn.commit()\n\n        # Verify inserts succeeded\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM reminder_history;\")\n        assert cursor.fetchone()[\"count\"] == 3, \"3 valid inserts should succeed\"\n\n        # Attempt invalid operation_status - should fail\n        with pytest.raises(Exception) as exc_info:\n            conn.execute(\n                \"\"\"INSERT INTO reminder_history\n                (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status)\n                VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'), ?)\"\"\",\n                (\"test-session-check\", \"hash-invalid\", \"invalid_status\"),\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n\n        conn.close()\n        assert \"CHECK constraint failed\" in str(exc_info.value), \"Invalid operation_status should fail CHECK constraint\"\n\n    def test_reminder_history_indexes_exist(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify all 3 indexes are created.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='index' AND tbl_name='reminder_history';\"\n        )\n        indexes = {row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n        conn.close()\n\n        expected_indexes = {\n            \"idx_reminder_history_session_hash\",\n            \"idx_reminder_history_shown_at\",\n            \"idx_reminder_history_session_tool\",\n        }\n        assert expected_indexes.issubset(indexes), \"All 3 indexes should exist\"\n\n    def test_reminder_history_index_columns(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify index column definitions.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n\n        # Check idx_reminder_history_session_hash (composite: session_id, reminder_hash)\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA index_info(idx_reminder_history_session_hash);\")\n        columns = [row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n        assert columns == [\"session_id\", \"reminder_hash\"], \"session_hash index should cover (session_id, reminder_hash)\"\n\n        # Check idx_reminder_history_shown_at\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA index_info(idx_reminder_history_shown_at);\")\n        columns = [row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n        assert columns == [\"shown_at\"], \"shown_at index should cover shown_at column\"\n\n        # Check idx_reminder_history_session_tool (composite: session_id, tool_name)\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA index_info(idx_reminder_history_session_tool);\")\n        columns = [row[\"name\"] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n        assert columns == [\"session_id\", \"tool_name\"], \"session_tool index should cover (session_id, tool_name)\"\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def test_reminder_history_cascade_delete(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Verify CASCADE delete behavior when session is deleted.\"\"\"\n        conn = storage._connect()\n\n        # Insert a test session\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"INSERT INTO scribe_sessions\n            (session_id, mode, started_at, last_active_at)\n            VALUES (?, 'sentinel', datetime('now'), datetime('now'))\"\"\",\n            (\"test-session-cascade\",),\n        )\n\n        # Insert reminder history entries for this session\n        for i in range(3):\n            conn.execute(\n                \"\"\"INSERT INTO reminder_history\n                (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status)\n                VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'), 'neutral')\"\"\",\n                (\"test-session-cascade\", f\"hash-{i}\"),\n            )\n        conn.commit()\n\n        # Verify reminder entries exist\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ?;\",\n            (\"test-session-cascade\",),\n        )\n        assert cursor.fetchone()[\"count\"] == 3, \"3 reminder entries should exist\"\n\n        # Delete the session\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM scribe_sessions WHERE session_id = ?;\", (\"test-session-cascade\",))\n        conn.commit()\n\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tests/test_reminder_history_schema.py", "byte_size": 11047, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 2, "line_count": 276, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tests/test_reminder_history_schema.py", "sha256": "3041bfc4add9828cd572f8a07f3b25518e08229fd3da722c9ca4bb06c5208f05"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T18:06:34.776604Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 18:06:47 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 6210, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Test suite for reminder storage methods.\n\nTests the three storage methods added for reminder history tracking:\n- upsert_reminder_shown() - Record reminder display\n- check_reminder_cooldown() - Check if reminder is in cooldown period\n- cleanup_reminder_history() - Remove old reminder history entries\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\n# Add the MCP_SPINE directory to Python path for imports\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.sqlite import SQLiteStorage\n\n\ndef run(coro):\n    \"\"\"Execute an async coroutine from a synchronous test.\"\"\"\n    return asyncio.run(coro)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef storage(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Create a temporary storage instance for testing.\"\"\"\n    db_path = tmp_path / \"test_reminders.db\"\n    storage_instance = SQLiteStorage(str(db_path))\n    run(storage_instance.setup())\n    yield storage_instance\n    run(storage_instance.close())\n    # Cleanup\n    if db_path.exists():\n        db_path.unlink()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef session_id(storage):\n    \"\"\"Create a test session and return its ID.\"\"\"\n    import uuid\n\n    # Create a session using the storage's session management\n    session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n    run(\n        storage.upsert_session(\n            session_id=session_id,\n            agent_id=\"TestAgent\",\n            repo_root=\"/test/path\",\n            mode=\"project\",\n        )\n    )\n    return session_id\n\n\nclass TestReminderStorage:\n    \"\"\"Test suite for reminder storage methods.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_upsert_reminder_shown_insert(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"First time showing reminder - should insert.\"\"\"\n        reminder_hash = \"test_hash_001\"\n        context_metadata = {\"test_key\": \"test_value\", \"count\": 42}\n\n        # Insert reminder\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n                project_root=\"/test/project\",\n                agent_id=\"TestAgent\",\n                tool_name=\"test_tool\",\n                reminder_key=\"test_reminder\",\n                operation_status=\"success\",\n                context_metadata=context_metadata,\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Verify insertion\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ? AND reminder_hash = ?\",\n            (session_id, reminder_hash),\n        )\n        row = cursor.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n\n        assert row is not None\n        assert row[1] == session_id  # session_id\n        assert row[2] == reminder_hash  # reminder_hash\n        assert row[3] == \"/test/project\"  # project_root\n        assert row[4] == \"TestAgent\"  # agent_id\n        assert row[5] == \"test_tool\"  # tool_name\n        assert row[6] == \"test_reminder\"  # reminder_key\n        assert row[8] == \"success\"  # operation_status\n\n        # Verify metadata JSON\n        metadata = json.loads(row[9])\n        assert metadata == context_metadata\n\n    def test_upsert_reminder_shown_update(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"Updating existing reminder - should update timestamp.\"\"\"\n        reminder_hash = \"test_hash_002\"\n\n        # Insert first reminder\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n                operation_status=\"neutral\",\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Get first timestamp\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT shown_at FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ? AND reminder_hash = ?\",\n            (session_id, reminder_hash),\n        )\n        row1 = cursor.fetchone()\n        first_timestamp = row1[0]\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Wait a moment\n        time.sleep(0.1)\n\n        # Insert again (should be new entry since we're using INSERT not UPSERT)\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n                operation_status=\"success\",\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Verify we have two entries now\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ? AND reminder_hash = ?\",\n            (session_id, reminder_hash),\n        )\n        row = cursor.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n\n        assert row[0] == 2  # Two entries for same hash (method name is misleading - it's INSERT not UPSERT)\n\n    def test_check_reminder_cooldown_within_window(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"Cooldown active - should return True (suppress).\"\"\"\n        reminder_hash = \"test_hash_003\"\n\n        # Show reminder\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Check immediately - should be in cooldown\n        in_cooldown = run(\n            storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n                cooldown_minutes=15,\n            )\n        )\n\n        assert in_cooldown is True\n\n    def test_check_reminder_cooldown_outside_window(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"Cooldown expired - should return False (show).\"\"\"\n        reminder_hash = \"test_hash_004\"\n\n        # Insert reminder with old timestamp\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO reminder_history\n            (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n            VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now', '-20 minutes'), 'neutral', '{}')\n            \"\"\",\n            (session_id, reminder_hash),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Check with 15-minute cooldown - should be expired\n        in_cooldown = run(\n            storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n                cooldown_minutes=15,\n            )\n        )\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tests/test_reminder_storage.py", "byte_size": 13012, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 407, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tests/test_reminder_storage.py", "sha256": "6d5e1ad3b82bf6c33d9328875e7dadc9ad301b3611f27c2983956c896f2907e3"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T18:06:47.130373Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

Check without any history\n        in_cooldown = run(\n            storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n                cooldown_minutes=15,\n            )\n        )\n\n        assert in_cooldown is False\n\n    def test_cleanup_reminder_history_by_session(self, storage):\n        \"\"\"Clean specific session only.\"\"\"\n        import uuid\n\n        # Create two sessions\n        session1 = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        run(storage.upsert_session(session_id=session1, agent_id=\"Agent1\", repo_root=\"/test1\", mode=\"project\"))\n\n        session2 = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        run(storage.upsert_session(session_id=session2, agent_id=\"Agent2\", repo_root=\"/test2\", mode=\"project\"))\n\n        # Insert old reminders in both sessions\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO reminder_history\n            (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n            VALUES (?, 'hash1', datetime('now', '-8 days'), 'neutral', '{}')\n            \"\"\",\n            (session1,),\n        )\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO reminder_history\n            (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n            VALUES (?, 'hash2', datetime('now', '-8 days'), 'neutral', '{}')\n            \"\"\",\n            (session2,),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Verify data was inserted\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM reminder_history\")\n        count = cursor.fetchone()[0]\n        conn.close()\n        assert count == 2, f\"Expected 2 reminders inserted, found {count}\"\n\n        # Cleanup with 7-day retention (should delete both)\n        deleted = run(storage.cleanup_reminder_history(cutoff_hours=168))\n\n        assert deleted == 2\n\n    def test_cleanup_reminder_history_global(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"Clean all old reminders.\"\"\"\n        # Insert old and new reminders\n        conn = storage._connect()\n\n        # Old reminder (8 days ago)\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO reminder_history\n            (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n            VALUES (?, 'old_hash', datetime('now', '-8 days'), 'neutral', '{}')\n            \"\"\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n\n        # New reminder (1 day ago)\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO reminder_history\n            (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n            VALUES (?, 'new_hash', datetime('now', '-1 day'), 'neutral', '{}')\n            \"\"\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Cleanup with 7-day retention - should only delete old one\n        deleted = run(storage.cleanup_reminder_history(cutoff_hours=168))\n\n        assert deleted == 1\n\n        # Verify new reminder still exists\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ?\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n        row = cursor.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n\n        assert row[0] == 1  # Only new reminder remains\n\n    def test_cleanup_reminder_history_respects_retention(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"Don't delete recent reminders.\"\"\"\n        # Insert reminder within retention window (6 days ago)\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO reminder_history\n            (session_id, reminder_hash, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n            VALUES (?, 'recent_hash', datetime('now', '-6 days'), 'neutral', '{}')\n            \"\"\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Cleanup with 7-day retention - should not delete\n        deleted = run(storage.cleanup_reminder_history(cutoff_hours=168))\n\n        assert deleted == 0\n\n    def test_reminder_cascade_on_session_delete(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"FK cascade deletes reminders when session deleted.\"\"\"\n        # Insert reminder\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=\"cascade_test_hash\",\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Verify reminder exists\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ?\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n        row = cursor.fetchone()\n        assert row[0] == 1\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Delete session\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        conn.execute(\n            \"DELETE FROM scribe_sessions WHERE session_id = ?\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        conn.close()\n\n        # Verify reminder was cascaded deleted\n        conn = storage._connect()\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM reminder_history WHERE session_id = ?\",\n            (session_id,),\n        )\n        row = cursor.fetchone()\n        conn.close()\n\n        assert row[0] == 0\n\n    def test_performance_under_5ms(self, storage, session_id):\n        \"\"\"All queries complete in <5ms.\"\"\"\n        reminder_hash = \"perf_test_hash\"\n\n        # Warm up\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Benchmark upsert\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        run(\n            storage.upsert_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n            )\n        )\n        upsert_time = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000\n\n        # Benchmark check\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        run(\n            storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n            )\n        )\n        check_time = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000\n\n        # Benchmark cleanup\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        run(storage.cleanup_reminder_history(cutoff_hours=168))\n        cleanup_time = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000\n\n        # All operations should be under 50ms (async overhead with thread pool)\n        assert upsert_time < 50.0, f\"upsert took {upsert_time:.2f}ms\"\n        assert check_time < 50.0, f\"check took {check_time:.2f}ms\"\n        assert cleanup_time < 50.0, f\"cleanup took {cleanup_time:.2f}ms\"\n", "line_end": 407, "line_start": 200}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tests/test_reminder_storage.py", "byte_size": 13012, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 407, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tests/test_reminder_storage.py", "sha256": "6d5e1ad3b82bf6c33d9328875e7dadc9ad301b3611f27c2983956c896f2907e3"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T18:06:59.810881Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

77717, "content": "    async def upsert_reminder_shown(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        reminder_hash: str,\n        project_root: Optional[str] = None,\n        agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        tool_name: Optional[str] = None,\n        reminder_key: Optional[str] = None,\n        operation_status: str = \"neutral\",\n        context_metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record that a reminder was shown in a session.\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier (FK to scribe_sessions)\n            reminder_hash: Hash identifying the reminder\n            project_root: Optional project root path\n            agent_id: Optional agent identifier\n            tool_name: Optional tool name (e.g., 'append_entry')\n            reminder_key: Optional reminder key (e.g., 'log_warning')\n            operation_status: Operation status ('success', 'failure', 'neutral')\n            context_metadata: Optional context metadata as dict\n        \"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            context_json = json.dumps(context_metadata or {}, sort_keys=True)\n            await self._execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO reminder_history\n                (session_id, reminder_hash, project_root, agent_id, tool_name,\n                 reminder_key, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, ?, ?);\n                \"\"\",\n                (\n                    session_id,\n                    reminder_hash,\n                    project_root,\n                    agent_id,\n                    tool_name,\n                    reminder_key,\n                    operation_status,\n                    context_json,\n                ),\n            )\n\n    async def check_reminder_cooldown(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        reminder_hash: str,\n        cooldown_minutes: int = 15,\n    ) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a reminder is within its cooldown period for a session.\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier\n            reminder_hash: Hash identifying the reminder\n            cooldown_minutes: Cooldown period in minutes (default: 15)\n\n        Returns:\n            True if reminder was shown within cooldown window (suppress),\n            False if reminder can be shown (cooldown expired or never shown)\n        \"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        cutoff_time = f\"datetime('now', '-{cooldown_minutes} minutes')\"\n\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            f\"\"\"\n            SELECT COUNT(*) as count\n            FROM reminder_history\n            WHERE session_id = ?\n              AND reminder_hash = ?\n              AND shown_at > {cutoff_time};\n            \"\"\",\n            (session_id, reminder_hash),\n        )\n\n        return (row[\"count\"] if row else 0) > 0\n\n    async def cleanup_reminder_history(\n        self,\n        cutoff_hours: int = 168,\n    ) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Delete old reminder history entries.\n\n        Args:\n            cutoff_hours: Delete entries older than this (default: 168 = 7 days)\n\n        Returns:\n            Number of entries deleted\n        \"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            # Use direct connection since _execute doesn't return cursor\n            def _cleanup_sync():\n                conn = self._connect()\n                try:\n                    cursor = conn.execute(\n                        \"\"\"\n                        DELETE FROM reminder_history\n                        WHERE shown_at < datetime('now', ?    ' hours');\n                        \"\"\",\n                        (f\"-{cutoff_hours}\",),\n                    )\n                    deleted = cursor.rowcount\n                    conn.commit()\n                    return deleted\n                finally:\n                    conn.close()\n\n            return await asyncio.to_thread(_cleanup_sync)\n", "line_end": 1910, "line_start": 1801}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 81645, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 10, "line_count": 1910, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "7cff58ea97bdfeaddc2b07f0163a25247de47a5dc2d12b9accca0a02e1a3ddd5"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T18:07:08.095913Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 18:07:23 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 7519, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Tests for session-aware reminder hash generation.\"\"\"\n\nimport pytest\nfrom unittest.mock import patch, Mock\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_engine import ReminderEngine, ReminderContext\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.settings import settings\n\n\nclass TestReminderHashSession:\n    \"\"\"Test session-aware hash generation with feature flag.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_hash_without_session_id(self):\n        \"\"\"Legacy format when session_id not provided.\"\"\"\n        engine = ReminderEngine()\n        context = ReminderContext(\n            tool_name=\"append_entry\",\n            project_name=\"test_project\",\n            project_root=\"/home/test\",\n            agent_id=\"test_agent\",\n            session_id=None,  # No session\n            total_entries=10,\n            minutes_since_log=5.0,\n            last_log_time=None,\n            docs_status={},\n            docs_changed=[],\n            current_phase=None,\n            session_age_minutes=None\n        )\n\n        # Build variables to get the hash\n        variables = engine._build_variables(context)\n\n        # Should use legacy format (no session_id in hash)\n        hash1 = engine._get_reminder_hash(\"test_reminder\", variables)\n\n        # Hash should be consistent\n        hash2 = engine._get_reminder_hash(\"test_reminder\", variables)\n        assert hash1 == hash2\n\n    def test_hash_with_session_id_flag_off(self):\n        \"\"\"Feature flag OFF = legacy format even with session_id provided.\"\"\"\n        # Create mock settings with flag OFF\n        mock_settings = Mock()\n        mock_settings.use_session_aware_hashes = False\n\n        with patch('scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_engine.settings', mock_settings):\n            engine = ReminderEngine()\n\n            context = ReminderContext(\n                tool_name=\"append_entry\",\n                project_name=\"test_project\",\n                project_root=\"/home/test\",\n                agent_id=\"test_agent\",\n                session_id=\"session_123\",  # Provided but flag OFF\n                total_entries=10,\n                minutes_since_log=5.0,\n                last_log_time=None,\n                docs_status={},\n                docs_changed=[],\n                current_phase=None,\n                session_age_minutes=None\n            )\n\n            variables_with_session = engine._build_variables(context)\n            hash_with_session = engine._get_reminder_hash(\"test_reminder\", variables_with_session)\n\n            # Create context without session_id\n            context_no_session = ReminderContext(\n                tool_name=\"append_entry\",\n                project_name=\"test_project\",\n                project_root=\"/home/test\",\n                agent_id=\"test_agent\",\n                session_id=None,\n                total_entries=10,\n                minutes_since_log=5.0,\n                last_log_time=None,\n                docs_status={},\n                docs_changed=[],\n                current_phase=None,\n                session_age_minutes=None\n            )\n\n            variables_no_session = engine._build_variables(context_no_session)\n            hash_no_session = engine._get_reminder_hash(\"test_reminder\", variables_no_session)\n\n            # Should produce same hash (legacy format ignores session_id)\n            assert hash_with_session == hash_no_session\n\n    def test_hash_with_session_id_flag_on(self):\n        \"\"\"Feature flag ON = new format with session_id in hash.\"\"\"\n        #
